## Text Elements
Q3. Your friend claims that he/she counted at least 5200 heads in a total of 10000 independent coin tosses. We saw in class as to why your friend's claim is (most probably) a joke. Present an argument for why this is (most likely) a joke using
 (1) Gaussian tail bounds, and 
(2) Chebyshev's inequality. 
In other words, write down upper bounds on the probability of having at least 5200 heads.  ^dy8lbBob

=3.34575564\times10^{-5} ^U7jQ1i7A
